{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017Rf4Aj5jd2jMhsgHtfhqwJ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"W1 reviewer response: feature correlation matrix + permutation importance.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport pandas as pd\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\\\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\\\nfrom sklearn.inspection import permutation_importance\\\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\\\\n\\\\nFEATURES = [\\\\n    \\\\\\\"mean_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"skewness_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"p10_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p25_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p50_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p75_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"mean_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"entropy_of_surprisal\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"type_token_ratio\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"hapax_ratio\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"bigram_entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"trigram_entropy\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"rep_2gram\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"rep_3gram\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"rep_4gram\\\\\\\",\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nCELLS = {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"pythia_nucleus095\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"data/features_all.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"pythia_temp09\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"data/pythia_temp09/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"pythia_topk50\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"results/pythia_topk50/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gpt2xl_nucleus095\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gpt2xl_temp09\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"results/gpt2xl_temp09/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"gpt2xl_topk50\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"results/gpt2xl_topk50/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"olmo_nucleus095\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"data/olmo/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"olmo_temp09\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"olmo_topk50\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\\\\\\\",\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nROOT = Path(\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_cell(path):\\\\n    rows = []\\\\n    with open(path) as f:\\\\n        for line in f:\\\\n            rows.append(json.loads(line))\\\\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\\\\n    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\\\\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=np.finfo(np.float64).max / 10,\\\\n                      neginf=np.finfo(np.float64).min / 10)\\\\n    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\\\\n    y = df[\\\\\\\"depth\\\\\\\"].values\\\\n    return X, y, df[FEATURES]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef part_a_correlation():\\\\n    _, _, df = load_cell(ROOT / CELLS[\\\\\\\"pythia_nucleus095\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    corr = df.corr(method=\\\\\\\"pearson\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    high_pairs = []\\\\n    for i in range(len(FEATURES)):\\\\n        for j in range(i + 1, len(FEATURES)):\\\\n            r = corr.iloc[i, j]\\\\n            if abs(r) > 0.8:\\\\n                high_pairs.append({\\\\n                    \\\\\\\"f1\\\\\\\": FEATURES[i],\\\\n                    \\\\\\\"f2\\\\\\\": FEATURES[j],\\\\n                    \\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\": round(float(r), 4),\\\\n                })\\\\n    high_pairs.sort(key=lambda x: -abs(x[\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\"]))\\\\n\\\\n    result = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"cell\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"pythia_nucleus095\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"correlation_matrix\\\\\\\": {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"features\\\\\\\": FEATURES,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"matrix\\\\\\\": [[round(float(corr.iloc[i, j]), 4) for j in range(19)] for i in range(19)],\\\\n        },\\\\n        \\\\\\\"high_corr_pairs_abs_gt_0.8\\\\\\\": high_pairs,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_high_corr_pairs\\\\\\\": len(high_pairs),\\\\n    }\\\\n    return result\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef part_b_permutation_importance():\\\\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\\\\n    all_results = {}\\\\n\\\\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\\\\n        X, y, _ = load_cell(ROOT / rel_path)\\\\n        n_features = X.shape[1]\\\\n\\\\n        perm_imp_folds = np.zeros((5, n_features))\\\\n        gini_imp_folds = np.zeros((5, n_features))\\\\n\\\\n        for fold_idx, (train_idx, test_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(X, y)):\\\\n            X_train, X_test = X[train_idx], X[test_idx]\\\\n            y_train, y_test = y[train_idx], y[test_idx]\\\\n\\\\n            rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\\\\n            rf.fit(X_train, y_train)\\\\n\\\\n            gini_imp_folds[fold_idx] = rf.feature_importances_\\\\n\\\\n            perm_result = permutation_importance(\\\\n                rf, X_test, y_test,\\\\n                n_repeats=10, random_state=42, scoring=\\\\\\\"accuracy\\\\\\\", n_jobs=-1,\\\\n            )\\\\n            perm_imp_folds[fold_idx] = perm_result.importances_mean\\\\n\\\\n        mean_perm = perm_imp_folds.mean(axis=0)\\\\n        mean_gini = gini_imp_folds.mean(axis=0)\\\\n\\\\n        perm_rank = np.argsort(-mean_perm)\\\\n        gini_rank = np.argsort(-mean_gini)\\\\n\\\\n        perm_ranking = [(FEATURES[i], round(float(mean_perm[i]), 6)) for i in perm_rank]\\\\n        gini_ranking = [(FEATURES[i], round(float(mean_gini[i]), 6)) for i in gini_rank]\\\\n\\\\n        perm_rank_arr = np.empty(n_features, dtype=int)\\\\n        gini_rank_arr = np.empty(n_features, dtype=int)\\\\n        for rank, idx in enumerate(perm_rank):\\\\n            perm_rank_arr[idx] = rank\\\\n        for rank, idx in enumerate(gini_rank):\\\\n            gini_rank_arr[idx] = rank\\\\n\\\\n        rho, pval = spearmanr(perm_rank_arr, gini_rank_arr)\\\\n\\\\n        perm_top5 = [FEATURES[i] for i in perm_rank[:5]]\\\\n        gini_top5 = [FEATURES[i] for i in gini_rank[:5]]\\\\n\\\\n        p90_perm_rank = int(perm_rank_arr[FEATURES.index(\\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\")])\\\\n        var_surp_perm_rank = int(perm_rank_arr[FEATURES.index(\\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\")])\\\\n        p90_gini_rank = int(gini_rank_arr[FEATURES.index(\\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\")])\\\\n        var_surp_gini_rank = int(gini_rank_arr[FEATURES.index(\\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\")])\\\\n\\\\n        all_results[cell_name] = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"permutation_importance_ranking\\\\\\\": perm_ranking,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"gini_importance_ranking\\\\\\\": gini_ranking,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"spearman_rho_perm_vs_gini\\\\\\\": round(float(rho), 4),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"spearman_pval\\\\\\\": float(pval),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"perm_top5\\\\\\\": perm_top5,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"gini_top5\\\\\\\": gini_top5,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"key_features\\\\\\\": {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\"perm_rank\\\\\\\": p90_perm_rank, \\\\\\\"gini_rank\\\\\\\": p90_gini_rank},\\\\n                \\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\"perm_rank\\\\\\\": var_surp_perm_rank, \\\\\\\"gini_rank\\\\\\\": var_surp_gini_rank},\\\\n            },\\\\n        }\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"  {cell_name}: spearman={rho:.4f}, perm_top5={perm_top5}\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\n\\\\n    return all_results\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef summarize(perm_results):\\\\n    p90_perm_top2 = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Jx3kxMdbnPAiASygiiLSid","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01M8RoiN3XmidhoJQMaR4QTd","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/feature_ablation_9cell.py","content":"# Feature ablation analysis across all 9 cells (3 models x 3 strategies).\n# Sub-tasks: A) Top-k ablation, B) Per-category ablation, C) Forward selection, D) Correlation + VIF.\n# RF params match existing pipeline: n_estimators=200, random_state=42, 5-fold stratified CV.\n\nimport json\nimport warnings\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom statsmodels.stats.outliers_influence import variance_inflation_factor\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=RuntimeWarning)\n\nROOT = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\")\nOUT_DIR = ROOT / \"results\" / \"feature_ablation\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nFEATURES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nCELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus095\": \"data/features_all.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"results/pythia_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus095\": \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"results/gpt2xl_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"results/gpt2xl_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus095\": \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n}\n\nCATEGORIES = {\n    \"perplexity\": [\"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n                   \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\"],\n    \"surprisal\": [\"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"],\n    \"lexical_diversity\": [\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\"],\n    \"ngram_entropy\": [\"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\"],\n    \"repetition\": [\"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"],\n}\n\nRF_PARAMS = dict(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\nSKF = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n\n\ndef load_cell(rel_path):\n    rows = []\n    with open(ROOT / rel_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if line.strip():\n                rows.append(json.loads(line))\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=1e15, neginf=-1e15)\n    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\n    y = df[\"depth\"].values\n    return X, y\n\n\ndef cv_accuracy(X, y, feature_indices=None):\n    if feature_indices is not None:\n        X_sub = X[:, feature_indices]\n    else:\n        X_sub = X\n    if X_sub.shape[1] == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0\n    accs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in SKF.split(X_sub, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(**RF_PARAMS)\n        rf.fit(X_sub[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        accs.append(rf.score(X_sub[test_idx], y[test_idx]))\n    return float(np.mean(accs)), float(np.std(accs))\n\n\ndef load_perm_importance():\n    path = ROOT / \"results\" / \"w1_permutation_importance_9cell.json\"\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    rankings = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        ranked_features = [item[0] for item in data[cell_name][\"permutation_importance_ranking\"]]\n        rankings[cell_name] = ranked_features\n    return rankings\n\n\n# === Sub-task A: Top-k ablation ===\ndef run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"=== Sub-task A: Top-k Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    ks = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        ranked = perm_rankings[cell_name]\n        cell_results = {}\n        for k in ks:\n            top_features = ranked[:k]\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in top_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[k] = {\"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                                \"features\": top_features}\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        full_acc = cell_results[19][\"acc_mean\"]\n        top5_acc = cell_results[5][\"acc_mean\"]\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: full={full_acc:.4f}, top5={top5_acc:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task B: Per-category ablation ===\ndef run_category_ablation():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task B: Per-category Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        cell_results = {}\n        for cat_name, cat_features in CATEGORIES.items():\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in cat_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[cat_name] = {\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"features\": cat_features, \"n_features\": len(cat_features),\n            }\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        best_cat = max(cell_results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: best={best_cat[0]} ({best_cat[1]['acc_mean']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task C: Forward feature selection ===\ndef run_forward_selection(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task C: Forward Feature Selection ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        selected = []\n        remaining = list(range(len(FEATURES)))\n        steps = []\n        for step in range(len(FEATURES)):\n            best_acc = -1\n            best_feat_idx = None\n            for feat_idx in remaining:\n                trial = selected + [feat_idx]\n                acc_mean, _ = cv_accuracy(X, y, trial)\n                if acc_mean > best_acc:\n                    best_acc = acc_mean\n                    best_feat_idx = feat_idx\n            selected.append(best_feat_idx)\n            remaining.remove(best_feat_idx)\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, selected)\n            steps.append({\n                \"step\": step + 1,\n                \"feature\": FEATURES[best_feat_idx],\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6),\n                \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"selected_features\": [FEATURES[i] for i in selected],\n            })\n        results[cell_name] = steps\n        n95 = next((s[\"step\"] for s in steps if s[\"acc_mean\"] >= 0.95 * steps[-1][\"acc_mean\"]), len(FEATURES))\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: 95% of full at k={n95}, full={steps[-1]['acc_mean']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task D: Correlation matrix + VIF ===\ndef run_correlation_vif():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task D: Correlation Matrix + VIF ===\", flush=True)\n    X, _ = load_cell(CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus095\"])\n    df = pd.DataFrame(X, columns=FEATURES)\n\n    corr = df.corr(method=\"pearson\")\n    corr.to_csv(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_matrix.csv ({corr.shape})\", flush=True)\n\n    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n    X_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X)\n    X_scaled_df = pd.DataFrame(X_scaled, columns=FEATURES)\n    X_with_const = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_scaled.shape[0]), X_scaled])\n\n    vif_data = {}\n    for i, feat in enumerate(FEATURES):\n        vif_val = variance_inflation_factor(X_with_const, i + 1)\n        vif_data[feat] = round(float(vif_val), 4)\n    print(f\"  VIF range: {min(vif_data.values()):.1f} - {max(vif_data.values()):.1f}\", flush=True)\n\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"vif_table.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(vif_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    return corr, vif_data\n\n\n# === Plotting ===\ndef plot_correlation_heatmap(corr):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))\n    im = Feature ablation analysis across all 9 cells (3 models x 3 strategies).\n# Sub-tasks: A) Top-k ablation, B) Per-category ablation, C) Forward selection, D) Correlation + VIF.\n# RF params match existing pipeline: n_estimators=200, random_state=42, 5-fold stratified CV.\n\nimport json\nimport warnings\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom statsmodels.stats.outliers_influence import variance_inflation_factor\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=RuntimeWarning)\n\nROOT = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\")\nOUT_DIR = ROOT / \"results\" / \"feature_ablation\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nFEATURES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nCELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus095\": \"data/features_all.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"results/pythia_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus095\": \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"results/gpt2xl_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"results/gpt2xl_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus095\": \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n}\n\nCATEGORIES = {\n    \"perplexity\": [\"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n                   \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\"],\n    \"surprisal\": [\"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"],\n    \"lexical_diversity\": [\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\"],\n    \"ngram_entropy\": [\"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\"],\n    \"repetition\": [\"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"],\n}\n\nRF_PARAMS = dict(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\nSKF = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n\n\ndef load_cell(rel_path):\n    rows = []\n    with open(ROOT / rel_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if line.strip():\n                rows.append(json.loads(line))\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=1e15, neginf=-1e15)\n    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\n    y = df[\"depth\"].values\n    return X, y\n\n\ndef cv_accuracy(X, y, feature_indices=None):\n    if feature_indices is not None:\n        X_sub = X[:, feature_indices]\n    else:\n        X_sub = X\n    if X_sub.shape[1] == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0\n    accs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in SKF.split(X_sub, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(**RF_PARAMS)\n        rf.fit(X_sub[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        accs.append(rf.score(X_sub[test_idx], y[test_idx]))\n    return float(np.mean(accs)), float(np.std(accs))\n\n\ndef load_perm_importance():\n    path = ROOT / \"results\" / \"w1_permutation_importance_9cell.json\"\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    rankings = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        ranked_features = [item[0] for item in data[cell_name][\"permutation_importance_ranking\"]]\n        rankings[cell_name] = ranked_features\n    return rankings\n\n\n# === Sub-task A: Top-k ablation ===\ndef run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"=== Sub-task A: Top-k Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    ks = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        ranked = perm_rankings[cell_name]\n        cell_results = {}\n        for k in ks:\n            top_features = ranked[:k]\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in top_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[k] = {\"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                                \"features\": top_features}\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        full_acc = cell_results[19][\"acc_mean\"]\n        top5_acc = cell_results[5][\"acc_mean\"]\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: full={full_acc:.4f}, top5={top5_acc:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task B: Per-category ablation ===\ndef run_category_ablation():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task B: Per-category Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        cell_results = {}\n        for cat_name, cat_features in CATEGORIES.items():\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in cat_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[cat_name] = {\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"features\": cat_features, \"n_features\": len(cat_features),\n            }\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        best_cat = max(cell_results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: best={best_cat[0]} ({best_cat[1]['acc_mean']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task C: Forward feature selection ===\ndef run_forward_selection(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task C: Forward Feature Selection ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        selected = []\n        remaining = list(range(len(FEATURES)))\n        steps = []\n        for step in range(len(FEATURES)):\n            best_acc = -1\n            best_feat_idx = None\n            for feat_idx in remaining:\n                trial = selected + [feat_idx]\n                acc_mean, _ = cv_accuracy(X, y, trial)\n                if acc_mean > best_acc:\n                    best_acc = acc_mean\n                    best_feat_idx = feat_idx\n            selected.append(best_feat_idx)\n            remaining.remove(best_feat_idx)\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, selected)\n            steps.append({\n                \"step\": step + 1,\n                \"feature\": FEATURES[best_feat_idx],\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6),\n                \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"selected_features\": [FEATURES[i] for i in selected],\n            })\n        results[cell_name] = steps\n        n95 = next((s[\"step\"] for s in steps if s[\"acc_mean\"] >= 0.95 * steps[-1][\"acc_mean\"]), len(FEATURES))\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: 95% of full at k={n95}, full={steps[-1]['acc_mean']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task D: Correlation matrix + VIF ===\ndef run_correlation_vif():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task D: Correlation Matrix + VIF ===\", flush=True)\n    X, _ = load_cell(CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus095\"])\n    df = pd.DataFrame(X, columns=FEATURES)\n\n    corr = df.corr(method=\"pearson\")\n    corr.to_csv(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_matrix.csv ({corr.shape})\", flush=True)\n\n    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n    X_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X)\n    X_scaled_df = pd.DataFrame(X_scaled, columns=FEATURES)\n    X_with_const = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_scaled.shape[0]), X_scaled])\n\n    vif_data = {}\n    for i, feat in enumerate(FEATURES):\n        vif_val = variance_inflation_factor(X_with_const, i + 1)\n        vif_data[feat] = round(float(vif_val), 4)\n    print(f\"  VIF range: {min(vif_data.values()):.1f} - {max(vif_data.values()):.1f}\", flush=True)\n\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"vif_table.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(vif_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    return corr, vif_data\n\n\n# === Plotting ===\ndef   .replace(\"bigram_entropy\", \"bi_ent\").replace(\"trigram_entropy\", \"tri_ent\") for f in FEATURES]\n    ax.set_xticklabels(short_names, rotation=45, ha=\"right\", fontsize=8)\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01V9dVaqwgzE89H1J1V1xtTm","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Feature ablation analysis across all 9 cells (3 models x 3 strategies).\n2\t# Sub-tasks: A) Top-k ablation, B) Per-category ablation, C) Forward selection, D) Correlation + VIF.\n3\t# RF params match existing pipeline: n_estimators=200, random_state=42, 5-fold stratified CV.\n4\t\n5\timport json\n6\timport warnings\n7\timport numpy as np\n8\timport pandas as pd\n9\tfrom pathlib import Path\n10\tfrom sklearn.ensemble import / \"feature_ablation\"\n18\tOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n19\t\n20\tFEATURES = [\n21\t    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n22\t    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n23\t    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n24\t    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n25\t    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n26\t]\n27\t\n28\tCELLS = {\n29\t    \"pythia_nucleus095\": \"data/features_all.jsonl\",\n30\t    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n31\t    \"pythia_topk50\": \"results/pythia_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n32\t    \"gpt2xl_nucleus095\": \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\",\n33\t    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"results/gpt2xl_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n34\t    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"results/gpt2xl_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n35\t    \"olmo_nucleus095\": \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\",\n36\t    \"olmo_temp09\": \"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n37\t    \"olmo_topk50\": \"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n38\t}\n39\t\n40\tCATEGORIES = {\n41\t    \"perplexity\": [\"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n42\t                   \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\"],\n43\t    \"surprisal\": [\"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"],\n44\t    \"lexical_diversity\": [\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\"],\n45\t    \"ngram_entropy\": [\"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\"],\n46\t    \"repetition\": [\"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"],\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\tRF_PARAMS = dict(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n50\tSKF = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef load_cell(rel_path):\n54\t    rows = []\n55\t    with open(ROOT / rel_path) as f:\n56\t        for line in f:\n57\t            if line.strip():\n58\t                rows.append(json.loads(line))\n59\t    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n60\t    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\n61\t    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=1e15, neginf=-1e15)\n62\t    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\n63\t    y = df[\"depth\"].values\n64\t    return X, y\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef cv_accuracy(X, y, feature_indices=None):\n68\t    if feature_indices is not None:\n69\t        X_sub = X[:, feature_indices]\n70\t    else:\n71\t        X_sub = X\n72\t    if X_sub.shape[1] == 0:\n73\t        return 0.0, 0.0\n74\t    accs = []\n75\t    for train_idx, test_idx in SKF.split(X_sub, y):\n76\t        rf = RandomForestClassifier(**RF_PARAMS)\n77\t        rf.fit(X_sub[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n78\t        accs.append(rf.score(X_sub[test_idx], y[test_idx]))\n79\t    return float(np.mean(accs)), float(np.std(accs))\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef load_perm_importance():\n83\t    path = ROOT / \"results\" / \"w1_permutation_importance_9cell.json\"\n84\t    with open(path) as f:\n85\t        data = json.load(f)\n86\t    rankings = {}\n87\t    for cell_name in CELLS:\n88\t        ranked_features = [item[0] for item in data[cell_name][\"permutation_importance_ranking\"]]\n89\t        rankings[cell_name] = ranked_features\n90\t    return rankings\n91\t\n92\t\n93\t# === Sub-task A: Top-k ablation ===\n94\tdef run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings):\n95\t    print(\"=== Sub-task A: Top-k Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n96\t    ks = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]\n97\t    results = {}\n98\t    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n99\t        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n100\t        ranked = perm_rankings[cell_name]\n101\t        cell_results = {}\n102\t        for k in ks:\n103\t            top_features = ranked[:k]\n104\t            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in top_features]\n105\t            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n106\t            cell_results[k] = {\"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n107\t                                \"features\": top_features}\n108\t        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n109\t        full_acc = cell_results[19][\"acc_mean\"]\n110\t        top5_acc = cell_results[5][\"acc_mean\"]\n111\t        print(f\"  {cell_name}: full={full_acc:.4f}, top5={top5_acc:.4f}\", flush=True)\n112\t    return results\n113\t\n114\t\n115\t# === Sub-task B: Per-category ablation ===\n116\tdef run_category_ablation():\n117\t    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task B: Per-category Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n118\t    results = {}\n119\t    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n120\t        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n121\t        cell_results = {}\n122\t        for cat_name, cat_features in CATEGORIES.items():\n123\t            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in cat_features]\n124\t            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n125\t            cell_results[cat_name] = {\n126\t                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n127\t                \"features\": cat_features, \"n_features\": len(cat_features),\n128\t            }\n129\t        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n130\t        best_cat = max(cell_results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n131\t        print(f\"  {cell_name}: best={best_cat[0]} ({best_cat[1]['acc_mean']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n132\t    return results\n133\t\n134\t\n135\t# === Sub-task C: Forward feature selection ===\n136\tdef run_forward_selection(perm_rankings):\n137\t    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task C: Forward Feature Selection ===\", flush=True)\n138\t    results = {}\n139\t    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n140\t        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n141\t        selected = []\n142\t        remaining = list(range(len(FEATURES)))\n143\t        steps = []\n144\t        for step in range(len(FEATURES)):\n145\t            best_acc = -1\n146\t            best_feat_idx = None\n147\t            for feat_idx in remaining:\n148\t                trial = selected + [feat_idx]\n149\t                acc_mean, _ = cv_accuracy(X, y, trial)\n150\t                if acc_mean > best_acc:\n151\t                    best_acc = acc_mean\n152\t                    best_feat_idx = feat_idx\n153\t            selected.append(best_feat_idx)\n154\t            remaining.remove(best_feat_idx)\n155\t            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, selected)\n156\t            steps.append({\n157\t                \"step\": step + 1,\n158\t                \"feature\": FEATURES[best_feat_idx],\n159\t                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6),\n160\t                \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n161\t                \"selected_features\": [FEATURES[i] for i in selected],\n162\t            })\n163\t        results[cell_name] = steps\n164\t        n95 = next((s[\"step\"] for s in steps if s[\"acc_mean\"] >= 0.95 * steps[-1][\"acc_mean\"]), len(FEATURES))\n165\t        print(f\"  {cell_name}: 95% of full at k={n95}, full={steps[-1]['acc_mean']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n166\t    return results\n167\t\n168\t\n169\t# === Sub-task D: Correlation matrix + VIF ===\n170\tdef run_correlation_vif():\n171\t    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task D: Correlation Matrix + VIF ===\", flush=True)\n172\t    X, _ = load_cell(CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus095\"])\n173\t    df = pd.DataFrame(X, columns=FEATURES)\n174\t\n175\t    corr = df.corr(method=\"pearson\")\n176\t    corr.to_csv(OUT_DIR / StandardScaler().fit_transform(X)\n181\t    X_scaled_df = pd.DataFrame(X_scaled, columns=FEATURES)\n182\t    X_with_const = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_scaled.shape[0]), X_scaled])\n183\t\n184\t    vif_data = {}\n185\t    for i, feat in enumerate(FEATURES):\n186\t        vif_val = variance_inflation_factor(X_with_const, i + 1)\n187\t        vif_data[feat] = round(float(vif_val), 4)\n188\t    print(f\"  VIF range: {min(vif_data.values()):.1f} - {max(vif_data.values()):.1f}\", flush=True)\n189\t\n190\t    with open(OUT_DIR / \"vif_table.json\", \"w\") as f:\n191\t        json.dump(vif_data, matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n200\t    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n201\t\n202\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))\n203\t    im = ax.imshow(corr.values, cmap=\"RdBu_r\", vmin=-1, vmax=1, ax.set_yticklabels(short_names, fontsize=8)\n211\t    cbar = fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, shrink=0.8)\n212\t    cbar.set_label(\"Pearson r\", fontsize=10)\n213\t    ax.set_title(\"Feature Correlation }\n278\t\n279\t    # Best category per cell\n280\t    best_cats = {}\n281\t    for cell_name in CELLS:\n282\t        best = max(category_results[cell_name].items(), key=lambda x: existing pipeline: n_estimators=200, random_state=42, 5-fold stratified CV.\n\nimport json\nimport warnings\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom statsmodels.stats.outliers_influence import variance_inflation_factor\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=RuntimeWarning)\n\nROOT = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\")\nOUT_DIR = ROOT / \"results\" / \"feature_ablation\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nFEATURES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nCELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus095\": \"data/features_all.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"results/pythia_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus095\": \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"results/gpt2xl_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"results/gpt2xl_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus095\": \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n}\n\nCATEGORIES = {\n    \"perplexity\": [\"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n                   \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\"],\n    \"surprisal\": [\"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"],\n    \"lexical_diversity\": [\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\"],\n    \"ngram_entropy\": [\"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\"],\n    \"repetition\": [\"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"],\n}\n\nRF_PARAMS = dict(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\nSKF = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n\n\ndef load_cell(rel_path):\n    rows = []\n    with open(ROOT / rel_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if line.strip():\n                rows.append(json.loads(line))\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=1e15, neginf=-1e15)\n    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\n    y = df[\"depth\"].values\n    return X, y\n\n\ndef cv_accuracy(X, y, feature_indices=None):\n    if feature_indices is not None:\n        X_sub = X[:, feature_indices]\n    else:\n        X_sub = X\n    if X_sub.shape[1] == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0\n    accs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in SKF.split(X_sub, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(**RF_PARAMS)\n        rf.fit(X_sub[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        accs.append(rf.score(X_sub[test_idx], y[test_idx]))\n    return float(np.mean(accs)), float(np.std(accs))\n\n\ndef load_perm_importance():\n    path = ROOT / \"results\" / \"w1_permutation_importance_9cell.json\"\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    rankings = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        ranked_features = [item[0] for item in data[cell_name][\"permutation_importance_ranking\"]]\n        rankings[cell_name] = ranked_features\n    return rankings\n\n\n# === Sub-task A: Top-k ablation ===\ndef run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"=== Sub-task A: Top-k Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    ks = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        ranked = perm_rankings[cell_name]\n        cell_results = {}\n        for k in ks:\n            top_features = ranked[:k]\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in top_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[k] = {\"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                                \"features\": top_features}\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        full_acc = cell_results[19][\"acc_mean\"]\n        top5_acc = cell_results[5][\"acc_mean\"]\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: full={full_acc:.4f}, top5={top5_acc:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task B: Per-category ablation ===\ndef run_category_ablation():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task B: Per-category Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        cell_results = {}\n        for cat_name, cat_features in CATEGORIES.items():\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in cat_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[cat_name] = {\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"features\": cat_features, \"n_features\": len(cat_features),\n            }\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        best_cat = max(cell_results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: best={best_cat[0]} ({best_cat[1]['acc_mean']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task C: Forward feature selection ===\ndef run_forward_selection(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task C: Forward Feature Selection ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        selected = []\n        remaining = list(range(len(FEATURES)))\n        steps = []\n        for step in range(len(FEATURES)):\n            best_acc = -1\n            best_feat_idx = None\n            for feat_idx in remaining:\n                trial = selected + [feat_idx]\n                acc_mean, _ = cv_accuracy(X, y, trial)\n                if acc_mean > best_acc:\n                    best_acc = acc_mean\n                    best_feat_idx = feat_idx\n            selected.append(best_feat_idx)\n            remaining.remove(best_feat_idx)\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, selected)\n            steps.append({\n                \"step\": step + 1,\n                \"feature\": FEATURES[best_feat_idx],\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6),\n                \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"selected_features\": [FEATURES[i] for i in selected],\n            })\n        results[cell_name] = steps\n        n95 = next((s[\"step\"] for s in steps if s[\"acc_mean\"] >= 0.95 * steps[-1][\"acc_mean\"]), len(FEATURES))\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: 95% of full at k={n95}, full={steps[-1]['acc_mean']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task D: Correlation matrix + VIF ===\ndef run_correlation_vif():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task D: Correlation Matrix + VIF ===\", flush=True)\n    X, _ = load_cell(CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus095\"])\n    df = pd.DataFrame(X, columns=FEATURES)\n\n    corr = df.corr(method=\"pearson\")\n    corr.to_csv(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_matrix.csv ({corr.shape})\", flush=True)\n\n    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n    X_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X)\n    X_scaled_df = pd.DataFrame(X_scaled, columns=FEATURES)\n    X_with_const = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_scaled.shape[0]), X_scaled])\n\n    vif_data = {}\n    for i, feat in enumerate(FEATURES):\n        vif_val = variance_inflation_factor(X_with_const, i + 1)\n        vif_data[feat] = round(float(vif_val), 4)\n    print(f\"  VIF range: {min(vif_data.values()):.1f} - {max(vif_data.values()):.1f}\", flush=True)\n\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"vif_table.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(vif_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    return corr, vif_data\n\n\n# === Plotting ===\ndef plot_correlation_heatmap(corr):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))\n    im = ax.imshow(corr.values, cmap=\"RdBu_r\", vmin=-1, vmax=1, aspect=\"equal\")\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(FEATURES)))\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(FEATURES)))\n    short_names = [f.replace(\"_ppl\", \"\").replace(\"_surprisal\", \"_sur\").replace(\"entropy_of_surprisal\", \"ent_sur\")\n                   .replace(\"type_token_ratio\", \"ttr\").replace(\"hapax_ratio\", \"hapax\")\n                   .replace(\"bigram_entropy\", \"bi_ent\").replace(\"trigram_entropy\", \"tri_ent\") for f in FEATURES]\n    ax.set_xticklabels(short_names, rotation=45, ha=\"right\", fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_yticklabels(short_names, fontsize=8)\n    cbar = fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, shrink=0.8)\n    cbar.set_label(\"Pearson r\", fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_title(\"Feature Correlation Matrix (19 features)\", fontsize=12)\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_heatmap.pdf\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_heatmap.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_heatmap.pdf/png\", flush=True)\n\n\ndef plot_forward_selection(fwd_results):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))\n    all_accs = []\n    for cell_name, steps in fwd_results.items():\n        ks = [s[\"step\"] for s in steps]\n        accs = [s[\"acc_mean\"] for s in steps]\n        all_accs.append(accs)\n        ax.plot(ks, accs, alpha=0.4, linewidth=1, label=cell_name)\n\n    mean_accs = np.mean(all_accs, axis=0)\n    ax.plot(range(1, 20), mean_accs, color=\"black\", linewidth=2.5, label=\"Mean\", zorder=10)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Number of Features\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_ylabel(\"4-class Accuracy (5-fold CV)\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_title(\"Forward Feature Selection (9 cells)\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_xticks(range(1, 20, 2))\n    ax.legend(fontsize=7, ncol=2, loc=\"lower right\")\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection_curve.pdf\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection_curve.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved forward_selection_curve.pdf/png\", flush=True)\n\n\n# === Summary ===\ndef build_summary(topk_results, category_results, fwd_results):\n    summary = {}\n\n    # Top-k stats across 9 cells\n    for k in [2, 5]:\n        accs = [topk_results[c][k][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n        summary[f\"topk_{k}_mean_acc\"] = round(float(np.mean(accs)), 6)\n        summary[f\"topk_{k}_std_acc\"] = round(float(np.std(accs)), 6)\n\n    # Full model baseline\n    full_accs = [topk_results[c][19][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n    summary[\"full_19_mean_acc\"] = round(float(np.mean(full_accs)), 6)\n    summary[\"full_19_std_acc\"] = round(float(np.std(full_accs)), 6)\n\n    # Features needed for 95% of full performance (per cell)\n    n95_list = []\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        full_acc = topk_results[cell_name][19][\"acc_mean\"]\n        threshold = 0.95 * full_acc\n        for k in [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]:\n            if topk_results[cell_name][k][\"acc_mean\"] >= threshold:\n                n95_list.append(k)\n                break\n    summary[\"n_features_95pct_full\"] = {\n        \"per_cell\": {cell: n for cell, n in zip(CELLS.keys(), n95_list)},\n        \"mean\": round(float(np.mean(n95_list)), 1),\n        \"max\": int(np.max(n95_list)),\n    }\n\n    # Best category per cell\n    best_cats = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        best = max(category_results[cell_name].items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        best_cats[cell_name] = best[0]\n    summary[\"best_category_per_cell\"] = best_cats\n\n    # Category mean across cells\n    cat_means = {}\n    for cat in CATEGORIES:\n        accs = [category_results[c][cat][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n        cat_means[cat] = {\"mean\": round(float(np.mean(accs)), 6), \"std\": round(float(np.std(accs)), 6)}\n    summary[\"category_mean_accuracy\"] = cat_means\n\n    # Forward selection: first feature chosen across cells\n    first_features = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        first_features[cell_name] = fwd_results[cell_name][0][\"feature\"]\n    summary[\"forward_selection_first_feature\"] = first_features\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import time\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    perm_rankings = load_perm_importance()\n\n    # A: Top-k ablation\n    topk_results = run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings)\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"topk_ablation.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(topk_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved topk_ablation.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # B: Category ablation\n    category_results = run_category_ablation()\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"category_ablation.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(category_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved category_ablation.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # C: Forward selection\n    fwd_results = run_forward_selection(perm_rankings)\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(fwd_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved forward_selection.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # D: Correlation + VIF\n    corr, vif_data = run_correlation_vif()\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation + VIF ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # Summary\n    summary = build_summary(topk_results, category_results, fwd_results)\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"summary.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Plots\n    print(\"\\n=== Generating Plots ===\", flush=True)\n    plot_correlation_heatmap(corr)\n    plot_forward_selection(fwd_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"\\nAll done in {elapsed:.0f}s. Results in {OUT_DIR}\", flush=True)\n","numLines":342,"startLine":1,"totalLines":342}}}

\"data/olmo/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"results/olmo_temp09/features.jsonl\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"results/olmo_topk50/features.jsonl\",\n}\n\nCATEGORIES = {\n    \"perplexity\": [\"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n                   \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\"],\n    \"surprisal\": [\"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"],\n    \"lexical_diversity\": [\"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\"],\n    \"ngram_entropy\": [\"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\"],\n    \"repetition\": [\"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"],\n}\n\nRF_PARAMS = dict(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\nSKF = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n\n\ndef load_cell(rel_path):\n    rows = []\n    with open(ROOT / rel_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if line.strip():\n                rows.append(json.loads(line))\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    X = df[FEATURES].values.astype(np.float64)\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=1e15, neginf=-1e15)\n    X = np.clip(X, -1e15, 1e15)\n    y = df[\"depth\"].values\n    return X, y\n\n\ndef cv_accuracy(X, y, feature_indices=None):\n    if feature_indices is not None:\n        X_sub = X[:, feature_indices]\n    else:\n        X_sub = X\n    if X_sub.shape[1] == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0\n    accs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in SKF.split(X_sub, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(**RF_PARAMS)\n        rf.fit(X_sub[train_idx], y[train_idx])\n        accs.append(rf.score(X_sub[test_idx], y[test_idx]))\n    return float(np.mean(accs)), float(np.std(accs))\n\n\ndef load_perm_importance():\n    path = ROOT / \"results\" / \"w1_permutation_importance_9cell.json\"\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    rankings = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        ranked_features = [item[0] for item in data[cell_name][\"permutation_importance_ranking\"]]\n        rankings[cell_name] = ranked_features\n    return rankings\n\n\n# === Sub-task A: Top-k ablation ===\ndef run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"=== Sub-task A: Top-k Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    ks = [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        ranked = perm_rankings[cell_name]\n        cell_results = {}\n        for k in ks:\n            top_features = ranked[:k]\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in top_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[k] = {\"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                                \"features\": top_features}\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        full_acc = cell_results[19][\"acc_mean\"]\n        top5_acc = cell_results[5][\"acc_mean\"]\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: full={full_acc:.4f}, top5={top5_acc:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task B: Per-category ablation ===\ndef run_category_ablation():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task B: Per-category Ablation ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        cell_results = {}\n        for cat_name, cat_features in CATEGORIES.items():\n            indices = [FEATURES.index(f) for f in cat_features]\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, indices)\n            cell_results[cat_name] = {\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6), \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"features\": cat_features, \"n_features\": len(cat_features),\n            }\n        results[cell_name] = cell_results\n        best_cat = max(cell_results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: best={best_cat[0]} ({best_cat[1]['acc_mean']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task C: Forward feature selection ===\ndef run_forward_selection(perm_rankings):\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task C: Forward Feature Selection ===\", flush=True)\n    results = {}\n    for cell_name, rel_path in CELLS.items():\n        X, y = load_cell(rel_path)\n        selected = []\n        remaining = list(range(len(FEATURES)))\n        steps = []\n        for step in range(len(FEATURES)):\n            best_acc = -1\n            best_feat_idx = None\n            for feat_idx in remaining:\n                trial = selected + [feat_idx]\n                acc_mean, _ = cv_accuracy(X, y, trial)\n                if acc_mean > best_acc:\n                    best_acc = acc_mean\n                    best_feat_idx = feat_idx\n            selected.append(best_feat_idx)\n            remaining.remove(best_feat_idx)\n            acc_mean, acc_std = cv_accuracy(X, y, selected)\n            steps.append({\n                \"step\": step + 1,\n                \"feature\": FEATURES[best_feat_idx],\n                \"acc_mean\": round(acc_mean, 6),\n                \"acc_std\": round(acc_std, 6),\n                \"selected_features\": [FEATURES[i] for i in selected],\n            })\n        results[cell_name] = steps\n        n95 = next((s[\"step\"] for s in steps if s[\"acc_mean\"] >= 0.95 * steps[-1][\"acc_mean\"]), len(FEATURES))\n        print(f\"  {cell_name}: 95%% of full at k={n95}, full={steps[-1]['acc_mean']:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    return results\n\n\n# === Sub-task D: Correlation matrix + VIF ===\ndef run_correlation_vif():\n    print(\"\\n=== Sub-task D: Correlation Matrix + VIF ===\", flush=True)\n    X, _ = load_cell(CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus095\"])\n    df = pd.DataFrame(X, columns=FEATURES)\n\n    corr = df.corr(method=\"pearson\")\n    corr.to_csv(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_matrix.csv ({corr.shape})\", flush=True)\n\n    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n    X_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X)\n    X_with_const = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_scaled.shape[0]), X_scaled])\n\n    vif_data = {}\n    for i, feat in enumerate(FEATURES):\n        vif_val = variance_inflation_factor(X_with_const, i + 1)\n        vif_data[feat] = round(float(vif_val), 4)\n    print(f\"  VIF range: {min(vif_data.values()):.1f} - {max(vif_data.values()):.1f}\", flush=True)\n\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"vif_table.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(vif_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    return corr, vif_data\n\n\n# === Plotting ===\ndef plot_correlation_heatmap(corr):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))\n    im = ax.imshow(corr.values, cmap=\"RdBu_r\", vmin=-1, vmax=1, aspect=\"equal\")\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(FEATURES)))\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(FEATURES)))\n    short_names = [f.replace(\"_ppl\", \"\").replace(\"_surprisal\", \"_sur\").replace(\"entropy_of_surprisal\", \"ent_sur\")\n                   .replace(\"type_token_ratio\", \"ttr\").replace(\"hapax_ratio\", \"hapax\")\n                   .replace(\"bigram_entropy\", \"bi_ent\").replace(\"trigram_entropy\", \"tri_ent\") for f in FEATURES]\n    ax.set_xticklabels(short_names, rotation=45, ha=\"right\", fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_yticklabels(short_names, fontsize=8)\n    cbar = fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, shrink=0.8)\n    cbar.set_label(\"Pearson r\", fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_title(\"Feature Correlation Matrix (19 features)\", fontsize=12)\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_heatmap.pdf\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"correlation_heatmap.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation_heatmap.pdf/png\", flush=True)\n\n\ndef plot_forward_selection(fwd_results):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))\n    all_accs = []\n    for cell_name, steps in fwd_results.items():\n        ks = [s[\"step\"] for s in steps]\n        accs = [s[\"acc_mean\"] for s in steps]\n        all_accs.append(accs)\n        ax.plot(ks, accs, alpha=0.4, linewidth=1, label=cell_name)\n\n    mean_accs = np.mean(all_accs, axis=0)\n    ax.plot(range(1, 20), mean_accs, color=\"black\", linewidth=2.5, label=\"Mean\", zorder=10)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Number of Features\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_ylabel(\"4-class Accuracy (5-fold CV)\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_title(\"Forward Feature Selection (9 cells)\", fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_xticks(range(1, 20, 2))\n    ax.legend(fontsize=7, ncol=2, loc=\"lower right\")\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection_curve.pdf\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection_curve.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved forward_selection_curve.pdf/png\", flush=True)\n\n\n# === Summary ===\ndef build_summary(topk_results, category_results, fwd_results):\n    summary = {}\n\n    for k in [2, 5]:\n        accs = [topk_results[c][str(k)][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n        summary[f\"topk_{k}_mean_acc\"] = round(float(np.mean(accs)), 6)\n        summary[f\"topk_{k}_std_acc\"] = round(float(np.std(accs)), 6)\n\n    full_accs = [topk_results[c][str(19)][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n    summary[\"full_19_mean_acc\"] = round(float(np.mean(full_accs)), 6)\n    summary[\"full_19_std_acc\"] = round(float(np.std(full_accs)), 6)\n\n    n95_list = []\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        full_acc = topk_results[cell_name][str(19)][\"acc_mean\"]\n        threshold = 0.95 * full_acc\n        for k in [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 19]:\n            if topk_results[cell_name][str(k)][\"acc_mean\"] >= threshold:\n                n95_list.append(k)\n                break\n    summary[\"n_features_95pct_full\"] = {\n        \"per_cell\": {cell: n for cell, n in zip(CELLS.keys(), n95_list)},\n        \"mean\": round(float(np.mean(n95_list)), 1),\n        \"max\": int(np.max(n95_list)),\n    }\n\n    best_cats = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        best = max(category_results[cell_name].items(), key=lambda x: x[1][\"acc_mean\"])\n        best_cats[cell_name] = best[0]\n    summary[\"best_category_per_cell\"] = best_cats\n\n    cat_means = {}\n    for cat in CATEGORIES:\n        accs = [category_results[c][cat][\"acc_mean\"] for c in CELLS]\n        cat_means[cat] = {\"mean\": round(float(np.mean(accs)), 6), \"std\": round(float(np.std(accs)), 6)}\n    summary[\"category_mean_accuracy\"] = cat_means\n\n    first_features = {}\n    for cell_name in CELLS:\n        first_features[cell_name] = fwd_results[cell_name][0][\"feature\"]\n    summary[\"forward_selection_first_feature\"] = first_features\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import time\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    perm_rankings = load_perm_importance()\n\n    # A: Top-k ablation\n    topk_results = run_topk_ablation(perm_rankings)\n    # JSON keys must be strings\n    topk_json = {}\n    for cell_name, cell_data in topk_results.items():\n        topk_json[cell_name] = {str(k): v for k, v in cell_data.items()}\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"topk_ablation.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(topk_json, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved topk_ablation.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # B: Category ablation\n    category_results = run_category_ablation()\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"category_ablation.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(category_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved category_ablation.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # C: Forward selection\n    fwd_results = run_forward_selection(perm_rankings)\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"forward_selection.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(fwd_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved forward_selection.json ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # D: Correlation + VIF\n    corr, vif_data = run_correlation_vif()\n    print(f\"  Saved correlation + VIF ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s elapsed)\", flush=True)\n\n    # Summary (use string keys from topk_json)\n    summary = build_summary(topk_json, category_results, fwd_results)\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"summary.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Plots\n    print(\"\\n=== Generating Plots ===\", flush=True)\n    plot_correlation_heatmap(corr)\n    plot_forward_selection(fwd_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"\\nAll done in {elapsed:.0f}s. Results in {OUT_DIR}\", flush=True)\nSCRIPT_EOF"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":239,"cache_read_input_tokens":59324,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":239},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"688ad2e7-9cbd-4ff3-be27-a105281335b0","uuid":"f68994c3-7349-46b1-98bb-9cb3eaf5d86b"}
